Climatological association of Moyenne Garonne

Fonds Climatological association of Moyenne Garonne (ACMG), 1973-2000

History - Biography
The Climatological association of Moyenne Garonne was created in 1973 and
works on the study of the climate in connection with agriculture. It
intervenes on the middle Garonne zone which mainly encompasses the
Tarn-et-Garonne, the Lot-et-Garonne and part of the Gironde. It conducts
regular climate readings on these zones, meteorological studies and
concrete actions in the field at the service of farmers. A commercial
company named Agralis has been attached to the association since 2003

Association of industry and commerce for the environment
of Normandy


---


1. Zone of identification
1.1. Type of entity
Association.
1.2. Authorised form of the name
Association of Industry and Commerce for the Environment of Normandy.
1.3. Other forms of the name
As.I.C.E.N.
2. Zone of the description
2.1. Dates of existence
1977 to today.
2.2. History
The C.E.D.I.H.N. was created in 1975 by industrialists following the law
on waste. This association is oriented towards the study of dangerous
and non-dangerous industrial waste.
After the war, the reconstruction of the city generated an increase in
consumption and therefore in pollution.
Laws thus succeeded one another to regulate the plants.
Indeed, most of them discharged their waste directly into the Seine or
into the streams.
The A.S.I.C.E.N. was created, in this framework, by industrialists members
of the Movement of the enterprises of France (MEDEF) to anticipate the
environmental questions in relation to industrial waste.
These are regional inter-professional associations. Their vocation is to
bring together companies of all sizes around environmental, health and
safety issues.
2.3. Place
76, quai de Boisguilbert
80000 Rouen
2.4. Legal status
Association under the law of 1901.
2.5. Functions and activities
It is an information service created to answer the questions of companies
but also to help defend their interests in the regional bodies and
meetings.
It offers the possibility of assistance in the field as well as
preferential rates for their technical days, the "Thursdays of the
environment", and allows easier access to environmental agencies,
professional unions and consular bodies.
The Collective Carbon Action, launched in 2009 by the Upper Normandy
regional group "Carbon Strategy" (in collaboration with the Agency for
the environment and energy management (A.D.E.M.E.), the ASICEN and
professionals aims to bring together all the actors who have carried out,
are carrying out or are interested in the carbon approach.
The 3 priorities of the Collective Carbon Action are:
- amplify the GHG quantification, notably among SMEs;
- encourage companies to initiate action plans through financial support
for framing or implementation studies;
- animate, through an expert consultant, a collective appropriation of
the issues, objectives and solutions through a dynamic of cross
fertilisation of ideas and initiatives.
2.7. Internal organisation
1 employee.

- European Regional Development Fund (F.E.D.E.R.).
3.2. Type of relations
- A.D.E.M.E.: collaboration,
- F.E.D.E.R.: financial support.
3.3. Description of the relation
The A.D.E.M.E. and the A.S.I.C.E.N. lead a collective action favouring the
sharing of experiences between the companies of Upper Normandy. This
initiative is supported by the European Union through the European
regional development fund (FEDER).
3.4. Dates of the relation
2009.

Bretagne Vivante - Society for the study of the protection of nature in
Brittany


---


1. Identification
1.1 Type of entity
Association
1.2 Authorised form of the name
Bretagne Vivante - Society for the study and protection of nature in
Brittany1.3 Other forms of the name
SEPNB
2. Zone of the description
2.1 Dates of existence
1968 - today
2.2 History
The Society for the study and protection of nature (SEPNB) was created in
December 1968 in order to prolong the action of the "Geographical and
Naturalist Circles of the Finistère", formed in November 1953.
Founded at a time when ecology still concerned only a few initiates, the
SEPNB played a pioneering role by creating ornithological reserves on
the coast. Its field of action quickly extended to all the problems of
defence of the environment arising across the five departments of
historic Brittany.
In 1968, the SEPNB association was recognised as being of public utility.
In 1998, the SEPNB changed its name and became Bretagne Vivante-SEPNB.
In 2009, on the occasion of its 50 years, the association updated its
associative project, and equipped itself with a three-year action plan
setting out the actions it intends to undertake as a priority.
Carried by nearly 3000 members, many militants and about forty
employees, the association today manages a network of more than 100
protected natural spaces, distributed over the five departments of
historic Brittany.
2.3 Places
Headquarters
186 rue Anatole France, 29200 Brest
Local sections
Trégor-Goëlo, Concarneau-Trégunc, Crozon, Douarnenez, Pays de Morlaix,
Quimper-Pays Bigouden, Quimperlé, Rade de Brest, Fougères,
Rance-Emeraude, Rennes, Châteaubriant, Estuaire-Loire-Océan, Nantes,
Belle-Ile, Brocéliande-Lanvaux, Kreiz Breizh, Pays de Lorient, Pays de
Vannes
2.4 Legal status
Association of "law 1901" type
2.5 Functions and activities
Bretagne Vivante is an active regional association which is integrated
into scientific networks (European programmes) and institutional networks
(Regional and departmental commissions).
Purpose of the association:
To safeguard in the departments of Côtes-d'Armor, Finistère,
Ille-et-Vilaine, Loire Atlantique, Morbihan and possibly in the
neighbouring departments biogeographically part of the same natural
region as Brittany, the natural fauna and flora at the same time as the
environments on which they depend (rocks, landscapes, soils and waters)
when their conservation is required because of a characterised
scientific, economic or aesthetic interest;
To develop the taste and interest for the natural sciences, geography
and the protection of nature;
To carry out scientific studies;
To protect from extinction the threatened animal and plant species;
To ensure the protection of the environment by being competent and able
to intervene in different fields inseparable from and complementary to
the protection of nature (protection of species and spaces), traditional
field of action of the SEPNB: waste, the management of natural
resources, the use of space, urban planning and regional planning,
energy, the impact of developments.
Means of action of the association:
To obtain in ownership, by purchase, or failing that in rental, lands
and to erect them into reserves or natural parks;
To develop and enhance the different kinds of parks and reserves of the
association, to monitor them and to maintain them with the help of paid
or voluntary staff;
To create in these parks and reserves, or outside but within the zone of
influence of the society, scientific stations, museums, faunal or
botanical parks and to ensure their management with the help of paid or
voluntary staff;
To lead any action in court, to become a civil party, to claim damages
and interest in the interest of the conservation of nature;
To undertake all research, to conduct any survey, to give any opinion,
to pursue all studies relating directly or indirectly to all these
questions;
To operate one or more libraries of Natural Sciences and geography;
To publish periodically its bulletin "Penn ar Bed", memoirs, as well as
booklets, posters, leaflets, postcards, etc... relating to the study or
the protection of nature, if need be in collaboration with societies or
establishments pursuing in whole or in part similar aims;
To ensure environmental education missions for all types of publics;
To manage environmental education centres with or without accommodation
and catering;
To organise training actions;
To participate in different consultation bodies with the public
authorities.

Reference

Level of description
Fonds
Title
Society for the study and protection of nature in Brittany
History
The Society for the study and protection of nature in Brittany (SEPNB)
is an association created in 1968, recognised as of public utility in
1968.
Purposes and activities: Study of physical and economic geography, of
the natural sciences and of the conservation of nature in the regional
framework; creation of reserves, natural parks and scientific stations;
protection of wild flora and fauna and of sites; publication of works
in the quarterly review Penn ar Bed.
Since 1964, establishment of an interdepartmental research office whose
role is to promote research and inform local authorities on problems
relating to the planning of rural space, coastal tourism and the
conservation of nature.
In 1998, the association changed its name and became Bretagne Vivante -
SEPNB.
The association Bretagne Vivante is the manager of several natural
reserves in Brittany (Saint-Nicolas des Glénan, island of Groix, Venec,
marsh of Séné, Cap Sizun and marsh of Pen an Toul).

2.2. History
Created in 1968, the CREPAN has acted for more than fifty years for the
preservation of the environment in Normandy. Managed by volunteers, it
brings together individuals and local associations. The CREPAN is
officially approved for the Protection of the Environment, it mobilises
the public and local actors, trains young people and leads various
actions for the preservation of nature.

Collectif STOP EPR, neither at Penly nor elsewhere

1. Zone of identification
1.1. Type of entity
Association.
1.2. Authorised form of the name
Collectif STOP EPR, neither at Penly nor elsewhere.
2. Zone of the description
2.1. Dates of existence
2009.
2.2. History
The association was created in order to oppose in a pacifist way the
construction of the electronuclear reactor of the European pressurized
reactor type, called EPR, on the commune of Penly. It also acts on the
national territory or abroad.
2.3. Place
Rouen.
2.4. Legal status
Association under the law of 1901.
2.5. Functions and activities
The association informs the local populations and the elected officials
about the dangers of nuclear power. It tries to promote democratic local,
national and international debates on energy policies.
The association develops and animates collective and unitary public
actions.
Furthermore, it raises awareness of the use of renewable energies.
Finally, it works for the protection of the environment and raises the
question of the elimination of nuclear power.

    Departmental meteorological committee of Maine-et-Loire

Accession 128J

Fonds of the Departmental meteorological committee of Maine-et-Loire,
1876-1990.

Physical description
45 boxes, 82 registers, 1 notebook, 8 containers 14.40 [ml]
Biography or history
The departmental meteorological committee of Maine-et-Loire was created
by a joint order of the president of the general council and the
prefect of Maine-et-Loire on 5 December 1995, but a meteorological
committee (or Commission) has been operating in the department, in
various forms, for about 130 years. The day-to-day operation of the
committee is ensured by an office animated by the departmental delegate
of Météo-France, who is its technical and administrative secretary.
Presentation of the content
Papers of the Departmental meteorological committee, publications,
papers of Albert Cheux and of Georges Esnault, observations of storms,
observation notebooks, daily and monthly summaries, solar and lunar
halos, annual reports.
Holding institution
Departmental Archives of the Maine-et-Loire

Biography or history
Albert Piche (1840, Chartres-1907, Pau). Came in 1865 to Pau to be
treated. He then undertook research work in climatology, astronomy and
meteorology. He invented the atmismometer, which was adopted by the
Paris Observatory as an evaporimeter. He held, among other functions,
those of secretary then president of the Meteorological commission.

Information on the method of entry
Part of the Albert Piche bequest, handed over by the municipal library
of Pau in February 1981, complement gift January 1990
Presentation of the content
Documents emanating from the scientific activities of the Meteorological
commission.
Communicability
Free access
Holding institution
Departmental Archives of the Pyrénées-Atlantiques

France nature environment


---


1. Zone of identification
1.1. Type of entity
Association
1.2. Authorised form of the name
France nature environment
1.3 Other forms of the name
FNE, FFSPN
2. Zone of the description
2.1. Dates of existence
1968 - today
2.2. History
France nature environment (FNE) is the French federation of
associations for the protection of nature and the environment. Created
in 1968 under the name of French federation of societies for the
protection of nature (FFSPN), the federation was born from the will of
several regional and national associations for the protection of nature
to unite to multiply their forces, while each keeping its originality
and its autonomy.
Between 1973 and 1973 it mobilised for the safeguarding of the Vanoise
narcissus.
The federation was recognised as of public utility in 1980. It is the
spokesperson of a movement of 3500 associations, grouped within 80 member
organisations, in metropolitan France and overseas.
It became, in 1989, France nature environment (FNE). The logo of the
Federation is the hedgehog, its signature is "Wherever nature needs us".
2.3. Place
Paris
2.4. Legal status
Association under the law of 1901.
2.5. Functions and activities
France nature environment is the French federation of associations for
the protection of nature and the environment. It is as such the
spokesperson of a movement of 3500 associations, grouped within 80
member organisations, present over the whole French territory, in
metropolitan France and overseas.
The federation brings together 44 territorial associations (which are
often themselves federations of associations), 14 national associations,
which mobilise for a specific environmental cause, and 12 corresponding
associations.
France nature environment is also a member of the European Environment
Bureau, which brings together 140 European associations.
It has the objectives of:
    • "Stopping the causes of erosion of biodiversity and the
      overexploitation of resources"
    • "Protecting man and participating in democratic life" in the face
      of environmental health risks
    • "Changing the development model"
2.6. Reference texts
Statutes of the association.
2.7 Internal organisation
The association brings together militants, volunteers and/or about 46
employees

Public interest grouping Seine-Aval


---


1. Zone of identification
1.1. Type of entity
GIP.
1.2. Authorised form of the name
Public Interest Grouping Seine-Aval.
575 Detailed inventory https://ressources.histoire-environnement.org/Repertoire-d-archives-detaille-FNE-
    Conseils-d-administration-assemblees
1.3. Other forms of the name
GIP Seine-Aval.
2. Zone of the description
2.1. Dates of existence
2003 to today.
2.2. History
The scientific research programme Seine-Aval was set up in 1995 on the
Seine estuary. First concentrated on the environmental functioning of
the estuary, it evolved towards a finalised research programme.
In its extension, the Public interest grouping Seine-Aval was created in
2003 then, renewed in 2007 for 6 years and in 2013 for 8 years, in order
to have on the Seine estuary a perennial interface, capable of
strengthening the collaborations between researchers, managers and
decision-makers of the territory.
A public interest grouping (GIP) is a partnership structure which allows
the development of cooperations between public authorities and private
partners. The members decide to pool their resources for a determined
period and on precise missions.
The geographical limits of the GIP are fixed:
. upstream: by the Poses dam, the Interdisciplinary research programme
on the environment (PIREN) Seine being in charge of the issues upstream
of the basin;
. downstream: at the eastern third of the Seine Bay;
. at all the internal catchment basins of the estuarine system and their
environments.
2.3. Places
Regional pole of knowledge
115, boulevard de l'Europe
80100 Rouen
2.4. Legal status
Public establishment.
2.5. Functions and activities
The GIP Seine-Aval aims to transmit high value data.
It ensures, through its scientific pole, the project management of the
research actions of the Seine Aval programme and through its operational
pole, the project management of the applications of the research and the
transfer of the results of this research.
The GIP is called upon to contribute to the reflection engaged within
the framework of the Global Management Plan of the Seine Estuary to
which it brings its expertise and its operational know-how.
3. Zone of relations
3.1. Associated corporate bodies, persons or families
. The Seine-Normandy water agency
. the Grand maritime ports of Le Havre and Rouen,
. the regional councils of Lower and Upper Normandy,
. the general councils of Calvados, Seine-Maritime and Eure,
. the Union of chemistry industrialists - Normandy,
. the community of the agglomeration of Le Havre,
. the agglomeration community Rouen-Elbeuf-Austreberthe.
3.2. Type of relations
These 11 members ensure the steering and the financing of the GIP
Seine-Aval through a board of directors.

Institute for the mastery of risks576


---


History
Created on 1 July 2002, thanks to the support of 14 large industrial
groups, the IMdR-SdF is an association under the law of 1901 which
succeeds the Institute for dependability (ISdF, entity of the French
Movement for quality) by extending its field of competences to the
mastery of risks. By integrating within it the European institute of
cindynics (IEC) in the summer of 2006, the Institute becomes "Institute
for the mastery of risks - Dependability - Management - Cindynics"
called "Institute for the mastery of risks".
Its vocation is to help companies and public bodies to adopt, in the face
of risks, a preventive approach to correctly identify, evaluate,
quantify, prioritise, master and manage the feared events. This approach
claims to be systemic and global: systemic because the nature of the
malfunctions can be technical, documentary, organisational, human,
environmental; global because it must embrace all the functions of the
company (technical, finance, legal, ...).
The Institute for the mastery of risks rests on: the dynamism of about
twenty working and reflection groups (GTR), its studies conducted in the
form of multi-partner projects commissioned by industrialists, meetings
(in the form of breakfasts, half-days or themed days) and its biennial
congress of risk mastery and dependability named Lambda Mu, its Centre
for orientation, documentation and technical information (CODIT) which
welcomes and guides the members of the IMdR through all the specialised
documentation, the quality of its relations with its partners.

Accession no. 20080080577
Gift of archives of the Institute for the mastery of risks (IMdR)

Les Amis de la terre


---


History of the producer
The association Les Amis de la terre France was created in 1973 and is
co-founder of the International Federation of Friends of the Earth. Since
their foundation, Les Amis de la terre, cradle of the political ecology
movements in France, have not ceased to make known and fight against the
serious attacks on the environment: nuclear energy, massive use of
hydrocarbons, industrial agriculture, pollution of the air, water and
earth, genetically modified organisms, destruction of the world forest
and plunder of natural environments, while denouncing the social impacts
of these technologies in the North as in the South of the planet and
defending participatory democracy everywhere in the world.
In 2004, Les Amis de la terre - France, in addition to the actions of
their local groups which they coordinate and support, concentrate their
actions on the protection of the forest, the necessary reform of national
and international financial institutions, and support the action of the
collectives Sortir du Nucléaire, Réseau Action Climat and ATTAC of which
they have been members since the origin.

Accession no. 20050521578

Limousin nature environment


---


1. Identification
1.1 Type of entity
Association
1.2 Authorised form of the name
Limousin nature environment
1.3 Other forms of the name
LNE
2. Zone of the description
2.1 Dates of existence
1975 - today
2.2 History
The Limousin federation for the study and protection of nature (FLEPNa)
was born in 1975.
It joined the French federation of societies for the protection of nature
(FFSPN) from 1980.
Originally, it brought together twelve environmentalist associations of
Haute-Vienne, Creuse and Corrèze. Until 2015, 140 associations would join
it in total.
In 2000, it changed its name: the FLEPNa became Limousin nature
environment. In 2017, it grouped 39 associations.
2.3 Places
Headquarters: Ferme de la Loutre Les Vaseix
87 430 Verneuil-sur-Vienne
2.4 Legal status
Association under the law of 1901
2.5 Functions and activities
Authorised by the State to take part in debates on the environment,
Limousin nature environment plays a role of civil mediator by making the
militant voice heard in numerous Advisory commissions on nature,
landscapes, agriculture, hunting, natural or technological risks, health,
water management, energy.
LNE also has a sentinel role with regard to environmental law.
LNE manages an initiation centre to the environment intended for young
people and the general public: the Nature Centre "La Loutre". Structure
at the service of the environment, the Nature Centre is recognised as an
actor of environmental education which welcomes children for the day or
for short stays. The facilitators intervene in the school environment,
with the general public: nature Sundays, nature festival, nature walks in
summer on remarkable sites of the Limousin, design educational discovery
trails, take part in fairs and public festivals
Sources: website http://lne-asso.fr/

Observatory of oil spills

Title of the documentary unit: 236 J580 - Fonds of the Observatory of
oil spills.
Date of the documentary unit: 1968-2006
Physical description: Linear metres: 2.20 ml
Biography or history
This fonds, of a dissolved association, was deposited at the Departmental
Archives by Henri Girard, administrator, on 27 July 2006.
This association, under the law of 1901, was founded on 8 April 2000 at
the instigation of Dominique Voynet then Minister of Regional Planning
and the Environment, following the shipwreck of the oil tanker Erika, on
12 December 1999, having caused an oil spill.
Put into dormancy at the end of 2002, the association was dissolved in
August 2006 for lack of financing.
Information on the method of entry
Gift
Presentation
The fonds is organised in the following way: general administration,
personnel, accounting, actions of the official bodies, actions of the
OMN (generalities, thematic working groups, field work),
documentation).
Legal status
Legal status: Private archives
Communicability
Communicable
Holding institution: Departmental Archives of the Loire Atlantique

Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay


---


1. Zone of identification
1.1. Type of entity
Association.
.2. Authorised form of the name
Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay
1.3. Other forms of the name
SDN Berry - Giennois - Puisay
2. Zone of the description
2.1. Dates of existence
2013 - 2018
2.2. History
"Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay" is an association under
the law of 1901 founded in 2013, part of one of the 200 member groups of
the network "Sortir du nucléaire".
While the construction site of the nuclear power plant of Dampierre in
the department of the Loiret is completed (commissioning in 1980),
another plant project is being born at Belleville-sur-Loire in the
department of the Cher at an orthodromic distance of 35 km. An
association, the Association for the defence of the quality of life in
Val de Loire (ADQVL) opposing this latter project was then created at the
end of the decade 1973. Despite contestations, the project was retained.
The Nuclear power plant of Belleville commissioned its two operating
units in 1987 and 1988.
Around 1995 was created the "Committee Stop Belleville Stop Dampierre"
whose objective was to bring together all the people and associations
who wished the closure of the two plants, in particular because of the
porosity of the concrete of that of Belleville. From 2007, the
association gradually fell into dormancy, until the nuclear catastrophe
of Fukushima in 2011 which gave rise to a new awareness and a
remobilisation against nuclear power. Locally, demonstrations were
organised every Tuesday of that month of March 2011.
A new association was then founded on 11 June 2013 (declaration at the
Prefecture on 2 July and published on 27 July in the Official Journal),
by former militants of the "Committee Stop Belleville and Stop
Dampierre", from Bourges, the Yonne, the Loiret and the Nièvre. The
objective was to reconstitute a strong associative structure, to inform
and act in favour of an abandonment of the use of nuclear energy and
nuclear armed force. From its constitution, it was unanimously decided
to adhere to the national charter of the Network Sortir du nucléaire, in
particular to access its competences. Since then, numerous actions have
been carried out with this objective of the abandonment of nuclear
power, while proposing and favouring the mastery of energy, and the
development of other means of Electricity Production.
2.3. Place
Headquarters - postal address:
9 bis, chemin de la Taupinière
 Bourges
2.4. Legal status
 Association under the law of 1901.
2.5. Functions and activities
    •   "Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay" is an
        association under the law of 1901 which exercises its competence
        in the departments of the Cher, the Loiret, the Nièvre and the
        Yonne. It symbolically adheres to the network "Sortir du
        nucléaire" and is invited to the annual General Assembly.
        Although belonging to this network, SDN Berry-Giennois-Puisay
        keeps its autonomy to carry out various actions with as main
        objectives, the exit from nuclear power (the association
        considers that the danger represented by the operation of
        nuclear power plants and the use of nuclear weapons is real and
        serious, and that it tends to increase with the ageing of the
        plants, and the uncontrolled development of advanced techniques
        of nuclear weapons: hence, the demand for the programmed
        shutdown of the plants and the abolition of the nuclear
        arsenal), awareness-raising on alternative energies and the
        fight against environmental waste.
 More precisely, "Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay" has as
 aims:
        • "to federate locally the individuals, the associations and
        other legal persons who want to exit nuclear power,
        • to implement all the actions that the law authorises, for an
        exit from nuclear power according to the charter of the network
        "Sortir du nucléaire" annexed to the present statutes,
        • to inform the population on the dangers of the nuclear sector,
        as well as on the energy alternatives,
        • to favour renewable energies respectful of the environment and
        the means of mastering the demand in energy and electricity.
        • to fight against the pollution and the risks for the
        environment and health that the nuclear industry and the
        development activities and projects linked to it represent
        (creation or extension of basic nuclear installations,
        construction of high voltage lines, research and development
        programmes, etc.)
       • to defend in court all its members."581
 Consequently, the association carries out actions which claim to be
 popular. They are proposed by common agreement by the members of the
 board of directors, but they are submitted to the agreement of all the
 members, who can attend all the work of the Board of Directors. These
 actions are the following:
        • Organisation of demonstrations and participation in marches
        (notably, in front of the plants of Belleville/Loire and St
        Laurent-des-Eaux on the anniversary dates of Chernobyl and
        Fukushima, regional demonstration "FukuChinon" in autumn 2014,
        demonstration "the Cher walks for peace",
        European demonstration "Fermons Fesseinheim" on 26 April 2015),


---


581 Article 2 of the statutes of the association.
         •   Organisation of symbolic actions, called "reaction actions",
         • Realisation of water samplings (as in 2017 at Montsoreau
         within the framework of a collective action with the collective
         "Loire-Vienne"),
         •    Information and communication campaigns: clarification of
         the information, press releases, radio appearances,
         • Leafleting, notably at the roundabouts of Cosnes/Loire, Saint
         Satur and Bourges,
         • Raising awareness of local elected officials of the importance
         of bringing the nuclear debate back into a democratic framework,
         • Proposals to replace the energy produced from nuclear power by
         renewable energies (wind, Solar, Biomass...) and by developing
         the techniques tending to ensure "energy sobriety",
         • Legal actions: filing of a complaint against the Plant of
         Belleville for non-respect of safety rules and operating
         infractions, infractions noted by the Nuclear safety authority
         (ASN) during a control in the summer of 2014, filings of
         complaints through the national network (in 2015 for 36
         infractions and in 2017 for 46 infractions).
2.6. Reference texts
Statutes.
2.7 Internal organisation
"Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay" meets once a year in
General assembly. The association is articulated around a Board of
directors of 12 members.
Its current President is Françoise Pouzet.
"Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay" also counts about sixty
members.
3. Zone of relations
3.1. Associated corporate bodies, persons or families
"Sortir du nucléaire Berry - Giennois - Puisay" maintains the following
relations:
• Member of the network "Sortir du nucléaire" (adhesion to the charter of
the network, access to the competences, common actions, two member
administrators of the network).
• Participation and member of the collective "Sortir du nucléaire Loire
et Vienne" created in 2015 (meeting every two months and common annual
action) 582.
•   Peace movement (common demonstrations).


---


582 The collective "Sortir du nucléaire Loire et Vienne" is a macro
    regional group working for the exit from nuclear power. This
    grouping ambitions to pool and dynamise the reflection, the
    information and the actions around this objective. It is composed of
    the following structures: SDN Berry-Giennois-Puisaye
    around the plants of Belleville and Dampierre, the ACIRAD Centre and
    the Fukushima network based in
    Orléans, SDN 41 around St Laurent des Eaux, SDN 37 in Tours and
    Chinon, SDN 49 in the vicinity of
    Angers, the Poitevin coordination "Vivons sans nucléaire" around
    Civaux, SDN 73 and SNE 73
    around the food ionisation centre Ionisos, SDN 80 in the
    Deux-Sèvres and, SDN Pays Nantais.
•   Representation within the Local information commission (CLI).
• Common actions with the local associations (including "the Association
for the defence of the quality of life in Val de Loire" (ADQVL)).
4. Zone of control

environment (AHPNE).

ISAAR (CPF).

Regional union of Brittany for the environment
1. Identification
1.1 Type of entity
Association
1.2 Authorised form of the name
Regional union of Brittany for the environment
1.3 Other forms of the name
URBE
2. Zone of the description
2.1 Dates of existence
1973 - 1989
2.2 History
The Regional union of Brittany for the environment founded in 1973
obtained the approval under the title of environmental protection in
1984.
2.3 Places
At the home of the descendants of a former member of the association
Lanester
2.4 Legal status
Association under the law of 1901
2.5 Functions and activities
Under the title of the Regional union of Brittany for the environment,
an association is constituted between all the legal and natural persons
who will adhere to the statutes.
The Regional union of Brittany for the environment groups the
associations, movements, unions and all groupings, including the local
committees of broader movements, endowed with civil personality, which
aim to defend and enhance in the Breton departments, nature and the
particular living environment of Brittany.
To defend the specific quality of life in Brittany, this union will take
all the means at its disposal.
It will endeavour in particular:
to provide its members with serious documentation;
to put its members in relation with persons and bodies able to help them
solve a specific problem;
and when they themselves have exhausted the means at their disposal,
to represent its members before the public authorities if they request
it;
to create opinion movements in favour of the values it intends to defend;
finally to define a real environmental policy.

Testimonies and speeches of former ministers of the environment
Testimonies: The former ministers of the environment sont souvent auteurs /autrices
d’ouvrages, où ils ou elles relatent certains aspects de cette expérience…
Vous trouverez sur le site Internet https://ressources.histoire-environnement.org/ des
extraits de ces ouvrages actuellement, read by students d’AgroParisTech/Université
Paris Saclay et de Sciences Po Saint-Germain-en-Laye.
